to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}\|^{2}\!-\!\tilde{\varphi}^{*}(\frac{1}{\mu}Tx\!+\!{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}),

where
\tilde{\varphi}^{*}
is the Fenchel conjugate of the function
\tilde{\varphi}(y)=\varphi(y)+\frac{1}{2\mu}\|y\|^{2}
. Noting that
\tilde{\varphi}
is
1/\mu
-strongly convex, it then follows from
(
hiriart2004
, Theorem 4.2.2)
that
\tilde{\varphi}^{*}
is
\mu
-smooth. Also, in view of the
1/\ell
-Lipschtz continuous of
\varphi
assumed in Assumption
2
or equivalently the
(1/\ell+1/\mu)
-Lipschtz continuous of
\tilde{\varphi}
, one has
\frac{\mu\ell}{\mu+\ell}
-strong convexity of
\varphi^{*}
. Therefore,
\tilde{\varphi}^{*}(\frac{1}{\mu}Tx+{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}})
is a
\frac{\mu\ell}{\mu+\ell}
-strongly convex function of
{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}
. Also, both
\frac{\mu}{2}\|{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}\|^{2}
and
\frac{\mu}{2}\|\frac{Tx}{\mu}+{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}\|^{2}
are
\mu
-strongly convex functions of
{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}
. Therefore,
\mathscr{L}_{\mu}(x;\lambda,{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}})
is a
\left(\frac{\mu\ell}{\mu+\ell}+2\mu\right)
-strongly concave function of
(\lambda,{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}})
.

\blacksquare

With the above lemma, we are directed toward utilizing
\mathscr{L}_{\mu}(x;\lambda,{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}})
as a Lagrangian to design corresponding primal-dual dynamics. A preliminary work should be done to relate the optimal solution
x^{*}
of (
3.1
) to the saddle point of
\mathscr{L}_{\mu}(x;\lambda,{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}})
. This is shown in the following lemma.

Lemma 4.2

Consider the optimization problem (
3.1
) under Assumptions
1
-
3
.

(1)
Suppose there exists a point
x^{*}\in\mathbb{R}^{n}
and multipliers
(\lambda^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*})\in\mathbb{R}^{r}_{+}\times\mathbb{R}^{s}\times\mathbb{R}^{m}
such that

\displaystyle\mathscr{L}_{\mu}(x^{*};\lambda,{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}})\leq\mathscr{L}_{\mu}(x^{*},\lambda^{*};{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*})\leq\mathscr{L}_{\mu}(x;\lambda^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*})

(4.5)

holds for all
(x;\lambda,{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}})\in\mathbb{R}^{n}\times\mathbb{R}^{r}_{+}\times\mathbb{R}^{s}\times\mathbb{R}^{m}
. Then
x^{*}
is an optimal solution to the optimization problem (
3.1
).

(2)
Suppose
x^{*}\in\mathbb{R}^{n}
is an optimal solution to the optimization problem (
3.1
). Then there exist
(\lambda^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*})\in\mathbb{R}^{r}_{+}\times\mathbb{R}^{s}\times\mathbb{R}^{m}
such that (
4.5
) holds.

Proof:
(1) We show that
(x^{*},y^{*};\lambda^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*})
with
y^{*}=y^{*}_{x,{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.45206pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.20552pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.45206pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.35623pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.45206pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-2.2604pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.45206pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-2.2604pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}}
defined in (
4.3
) satisfies the
\mathcal{K}\mathcal{K}\mathcal{T}
system (
3.3
) so that
(x^{*},y^{*})
is an optimal solution to (
3.2
) and consequently
x^{*}
is an optimal solution to (
3.1
).

Firstly, we use the first inequality in (
4.5
), i.e.,

\displaystyle f(x^{*})+\varphi(y^{*}_{x^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.45206pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.20552pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.45206pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.35623pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.45206pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-2.2604pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.45206pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-2.2604pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}})+\lambda^{*\top}g(x^{*})+{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{\top}h(x^{*})+{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{\top}(Tx^{*}-y^{*}_{x^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.45206pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.20552pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.45206pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.35623pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.45206pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-2.2604pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.45206pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-2.2604pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}})+\frac{1}{2\mu}\|Tx^{*}-y^{*}_{x,{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.45206pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.20552pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.45206pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.35623pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.45206pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-2.2604pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.45206pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-2.2604pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\hfill\leq\mathcal{L}_{\mu}(x^{*};\lambda^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*}).

Noting that the right side of the above inequality is a constant and this inequality holds for all
\lambda\succeq 0
, we must have
g(x^{*})\preceq 0
. Similarity, this inequality holds for all
{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}
so that one must have
h(x^{*})=0
. Also, this inequality holds for all
{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}
, implying
Tx^{*}=y^{*}_{x,{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.45206pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.20552pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.45206pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.35623pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.45206pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-2.2604pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.45206pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-2.2604pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}}
.

On the one hand, taking
(\lambda,{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}})=(0,{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*})
in the first inequality in (
4.5
) gives
\lambda^{*\top}g(x^{*})\geq 0
; on the other hand,
\lambda^{*}\succeq 0
and
g(x^{*})\preceq 0
give
\lambda^{*\top}g(x^{*})\leq 0
. Therefore,
g(x^{*})\preceq 0
gives
\lambda^{*\top}g(x^{*})=0
.

Lastly, we prove
(x^{*};\lambda^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*})
satisfies (
3.3f
). With
y^{*}=y^{*}_{x^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.45206pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.20552pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.45206pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.35623pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.45206pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-2.2604pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.45206pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-2.2604pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*}}
and by referring to the right hand side of the inequality (
4.5
), one has

\displaystyle L^{\mu}(x^{*},y^{*}_{x^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.45206pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.20552pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.45206pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.35623pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.45206pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-2.2604pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.45206pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-2.2604pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*}};\lambda^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\nabla f(x^{*})+\sum_{i=1}^{r}\lambda^{*\top}\nabla g_{i}(x^{*})+\sum_{i=1}^{s}{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*\top}\nabla h_{i}(x^{*})+T^{\top}{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*}p\ni 0.

Therefore,
(x^{*};\lambda^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*})
satisfies the KKT system (
3.3
), implying that
(x^{*},y^{*})
is an optimal solution of (
3.2
) and consequently
x^{*}
is an optimal solution of (
3.1
).

(2) Suppose
x^{*}\in\mathbb{R}^{n}
is an optimal solution to the optimization problem (
3.1
) so that
(x^{*},y^{*})
with
y^{*}=Tx^{*}
is an optimal solution to (
3.2
), implying the existence of
(\lambda^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*})
such that
(x^{*},y^{*};\lambda^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*})
satisfies the KKT system (
3.3
). Therefore,

\displaystyle\mathscr{L}_{\mu}(x^{*};\lambda^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*})\leq L^{\mu}(x^{*},y^{*};\lambda^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*})=f(x^{*})+\varphi(y^{*}).

(4.8)

On the other hand, the strong duality for the problem (
3.2
) implies

\displaystyle f(x^{*})+\varphi(y^{*})

\displaystyle\!=\!\underset{x,y}{{\rm min}}\left\{f(x)+\varphi(y)+\lambda^{*\top}g(x)+{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*\top}h(x)+{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*\top}(Tx-y)\right\}

\displaystyle\leq f(x)+\varphi(y)+\lambda^{*\top}g(x)+{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*\top}h(x)+{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*\top}(Tx-y)

\displaystyle\leq\mathscr{L}_{\mu}(x;\lambda^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*}).

(4.9)

Combing the inequalities (
4.8
) and (
4.1
), one obtains
\mathscr{L}_{\mu}(x^{*};\lambda^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*})
which is the second inequality in (
4.5
).

To prove the first inequality in (

), we recall the KKT systems (

) and have the following inequality

\displaystyle\mathscr{L}_{\mu}(x^{*};\lambda,{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}})

\displaystyle\hskip 162.18062pt+(1/2\mu)\|Tx^{*}-y^{*}_{x^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.45206pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.20552pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.45206pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.35623pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.45206pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-2.2604pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.45206pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-2.2604pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=f(x^{*})+\varphi(y^{*}_{x^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.45206pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*\top}h(x^{*})\!+\!{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*\top}(Tx^{*}\!-\!y^{*}_{x^{*}\!\!,{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.45206pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.20552pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.45206pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.35623pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.45206pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-2.2604pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.45206pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-2.2604pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*}})

\displaystyle\hskip 162.18062pt+(1/2\mu)\|Tx^{*}-y^{*}_{x^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.45206pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.20552pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.45206pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.35623pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.45206pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-2.2604pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.45206pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-2.2604pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathscr{L}_{\mu}(x^{*};\lambda^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*}).

This completes the second part of the proof.

\blacksquare

4.2
Projection-free primal-dual optimization dynamics

We are now in a position to design a primal-dual dynamics building on the PAL (
4.4
) to seek the optimal solution
(x^{*},y^{*})
of the optimization problem (
3.2
) with exponential convergence. This algorithm excuses gradient descent on
x
and gradient ascent on
(\lambda,{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}})
. However, the gradient descent of
\mathscr{L}_{\mu}
on
\lambda
requires a projection onto the positive quadrant to keep the resulting
\lambda
dynamics stay non-negative; more specifically,
\dot{\lambda}=[\nabla_{\lambda}\mathscr{L}_{\mu}]_{+}
, which has a discontinuous right-hand side. In this paper, we propose a new design procedure for this Lagrangian dynamics without using projection but referring to mirror ascent; it comes with the advantage that the resulting dynamics is smooth so that its convergence analysis and numerical simulations are relatively simple compared with non-smooth projection based dynamics. More specifically, we propose the following projection-free algorithm which, for the PAL (
4.4
), performs gradient descent on the primal variable
x
, gradient ascent on the dual variables
({\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}})
, and mirror ascent on the dual variable
\lambda
, specified as follows,

\displaystyle\dot{x}=-\nabla f(x)-T^{\top}\nabla\varphi_{\mu}(Tx+\mu{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}})-\lambda^{\top}\nabla g(x)-{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{\top}\nabla h(x),

(4.10a)

\displaystyle\dot{\lambda}=[\lambda\oslash(1+\eta\odot\lambda)]\odot g(x),\lambda(0)\succeq 0,

(4.10b)

\displaystyle\dot{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}=h(x),

(4.10c)

\displaystyle\dot{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}=\mu\nabla\varphi_{\mu}(Tx+\mu{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}})-\mu{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}.

(4.10d)

Remark 4.3

Some remarks are given regarding the intuition behind the projection-free dynamics (
4.10b
).
Recall that the mirror descent algorithm is devoted to the constrained minimization problem
{\rm min}_{y\in\mathcal{Y}}\digamma(y)
, where

\mathcal{Y}
is a convex set in an Euclidian space. Let
\phi:\mathcal{Y}\!\rightarrow\!\mathbb{R}
be a function which is strictly convex and twice differentiable. Its conjugate convex function

\phi^{*}:\mathcal{Y}\!\rightarrow\!\mathbb{R}
is defined as

\phi^{*}(\omega)\!=\!\sup_{y\in\mathcal{Y}}\{(y,\omega)\!-\!\phi(y)\},

which can be shown as convex and differentiable
(
rockafellar1970
, Theorem 26.3)
.
Let
\mathcal{Z}\!=\!\{z|z\!=\!\nabla\phi(y),y\!\in\!\mathcal{Y}\}
be the image of
\mathcal{Y}
under the mapping
\nabla\phi
. Then
\nabla\phi:\mathcal{Y}\!\rightarrow\!\mathcal{Z}
and
\nabla\phi^{*}:\mathcal{Z}\!\rightarrow\!\mathcal{Y}
. The continuous-time mirror descent algorithm for the above constrained minimization problem takes the following form (
(
raginsky2012
, e.q. (5))
):
\dot{z}\!=\!-\!\nabla\digamma(y),y\!=\!\nabla\phi^{*}(z)
.
The second equation
y\!=\!\nabla\phi^{*}(z)
is equivalent to
z\!=\!\nabla\phi(y)
. Taking derivative yields
\dot{z}\!=\!\nabla^{2}\phi(y)\dot{y}
. Therefore the first equation in the mirror descent becomes
\dot{y}\!=\!-[\nabla^{2}\phi(y)]^{-1}\nabla\digamma(y)
.
Choosing the constraint set as
\mathcal{Y}\!=\!\{y|y_{i}\geq 0\}
and
\phi(y)\!=\!\frac{\eta}{2}\|y\|^{2}\!+\!\sum_{i=1}^{n}y_{i}\ln y_{i}
which is well defined on
\mathcal{Y}
, the above mirror dynamics can be written as

\dot{y}=-{\rm diag}[y_{i}/(1+\eta y_{i})]_{i=1}^{n}\nabla\digamma(y)
.
Letting
y=\lambda_{i}
and
\digamma(\lambda_{i})
be a function of
\lambda_{i}
defined as the right hand side of equation (
4.4
), then the above dynamics is exactly (
4.10b
).

Before carrying out the convergence analysis of the algorithm (
4.10
), the relationship between the equilibrium of (
4.10
) and the optimal solution of (
3.1
) is revealed in the following lemma.

Lemma 4.4

A point
(x^{*};\lambda^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*})\in\mathbb{R}^{n}\times\mathbb{R}^{r}_{+}\times\mathbb{R}^{s}\times\mathbb{R}^{m}
is the equilibrium of (
4.10
) if and only if
x^{*}
is the optimal solution to the optimization problem (
3.2
).

Proof:
Suppose
x^{*}
is an optimal solution. It follows from Lemma
4.2
that there exists
(\lambda^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*})\in\mathbb{R}^{r}_{+}\times\mathbb{R}^{s}\times\mathbb{R}^{m}
such that the saddle point inequality (
4.5
) holds. This implies that at
(\lambda^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*})
it holds
\partial\mathscr{L}_{\mu}(x^{*};\lambda^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*})/\partial x=0
,
\partial\mathscr{L}_{\mu}(x^{*};\lambda^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*})/\partial{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}=0
and
\partial\mathscr{L}_{\mu}(x^{*};\lambda^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*})/\partial{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}=0
. These three equations exactly shows that the right hands of the equations (
4.10a
), (
4.10c
), and (
4.10d
) vanish at
(\lambda^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*})
. The right hand of the equation (
4.10b
) also takes zero value at
(\lambda^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*})
in view of KKT subsystem (
3.3e
). Therefore,
(x^{*};\lambda^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*})
is the equilibrium of (
4.10
).

On the other hand, letting
(x^{*};\lambda^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*})
be the equilibrium of (
4.10
) and inserting it into (
4.10a
), (
4.10c
), and (
4.10d
), one obtains

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\mathscr{L}_{\mu}(x^{*};\lambda^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*})}{\partial x}=0,

(4.11)

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\mathscr{L}_{\mu}(x^{*};\lambda^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.72218pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.0mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-3.22916pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.5mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*})}{\partial{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}}=0,

(4.13)

Recall the fact that, for a differentiable convex (concave) function
F(x)
, a necessary and sufficient condition for
\bar{x}
to be the minima (maxima) of
F
is
\nabla F(\bar{x})=0
.
By using this fact, the result (
4.11
) gives the minima inequality

\displaystyle\mathscr{L}_{\mu}(x^{*};\lambda^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.93747pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\textstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar 22$}\hss}}\lambda}}^{*},{\mathchoice{\smash{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{-0.6458pt}{$\displaystyle\mkern 1.2mu\mathchar
